# Skills · A · part 3

931 entries, a11y-audit to a2a-communicator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **a11y-audit**: Audit a page for accessibility defects in an isolated headless chromium - axe-core plus custom color-contrast, focus-visible, tap-target (&gt;=24px), landmark and keyboard-reachability (real Tab walk) checks - across its reachable interactive states, rank findings by how much each blocks a screen-reader or keyboard-only user, and with --fix write a SAFELY-FIXED COPY (never the input) and report before/after violation counts. Use when accessibility problems should be fixed, not just pointed at. ([gsornsen/web-craft-skills](https://github.com/gsornsen/web-craft-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/a11y-audit/skills/a11y-audit))
